901 entries, roadmap to rocket-fuel. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **roadmap**: Use when the user wants to update the roadmap, build a Now/Next/Later view, reprioritize after new information, decide what moves to make room for a new initiative, shift timelines after a dependency slip, or figure out where a new initiative fits. - **roadmap**: Turn one big goal into a dated, sequenced, measurable ROADMAP.md you stick to. Trigger when the user wants to roadmap / scope out / plan / sequence a multi-feature goal, says "turn this idea into a plan", or keeps iterating with no deadline or win condition. A commitment device vs exploration-loop drift: frame -&gt; decompose -&gt; map deps/risks -&gt; sequence into time-boxed milestones -&gt; hand each feature to build-loop. Each milestone gets a deadline, done-criteria, and a hypothesis you score. Skipping it = endless iteration, no ship date. Single feature -&gt; use build-loop. ([GhostlyGawd/recursive-harness](https://github.com/GhostlyGawd/recursive-harness/tree/HEAD/skills/roadmap) · ★3)
